   --> 🧰️framework/🔨️modules/📡️replication/📦️packages/🦀️rust/./../../📡️wire/🦀️component.rs:420:92

    |                                    --------------------------------------------------------^ the trait `From<impl Future<Output = wire::codec::ProtocolError>>` is not implemented for `wire::codec::ProtocolError`
    |                                    |
    |                                    this can't be annotated with `?` because it has type `Result<_, impl Future<Output = wire::codec::ProtocolError>>`
    |

   --> 🧰️framework/🔨️modules/📡️replication/📦️packages/🦀️rust/./../../🧾️wire/🦀️component.rs:6:1
    |
  6 | pub enum ProtocolError {
    | ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
    = note: the question mark operation (`?`) implicitly performs a conversion on the error value using the `From` trait
help: the trait `From<impl Future<Output = wire::codec::ProtocolError>>` is not implemented for `wire::codec::ProtocolError`
      but trait `From<codec::component::PackError>` is implemented for it
   --> 🧰️framework/🔨️modules/📡️replication/📦️packages/🦀️rust/./../../🧾️wire/🦀️component.rs:8:10
